{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_01DeNCqx4NYES97FDzCdwVSN","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"thinking","thinking":"Server has ~1TB RAM, and the process was using only 1.3GB. So OOM kill is unlikely. Let me check what actually happened.\n\nLet me think about this differently. The process ran for 34 minutes at high CPU, then disappeared. Log is 0 bytes. No OOM. Possible causes:\n1. An unhandled exception (but stderr goes to the log which is 0 bytes - Python full buffering)\n2. The process received a signal (SIGKILL from outside)\n3. A segfault in numpy/BLAS\n4. The process actually completed but didn't write output due to a code bug\n\n\n5. Python's stdout buffering with nohup redirection could have prevented output from being written, but Python normally flushes on exit unless killed abruptly\n\nWith 1TB RAM available, OOM is ruled out. A segfault in numpy BLAS after 34 minutes of successful computation seems unlikely. Let me look more carefully at when the process actually started and stopped.\n\nThe log file's modify time of 18:46:35 is puzzling — that's only 19 minutes after the process started at 18:27:32, yet the last successful ps check showed the process running at 34 minutes elapsed. Either the file was truncated or reopened at that 19-minute mark, or the modify time reflects when nohup initially set up the redirection rather than when actual output was written.\n\nI'm realizing there were two separate processes involved — the initial ssh_execute run (PID 76849) that timed out, and then the nohup version (PID 77012). The first one wouldn't have been writing to the log file anyway since it wasn't backgrounded. Returns scalar in [0, 1].\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    X = X - X.mean(axis=0)\\\\n    Y = Y - Y.mean(axis=0)\\\\n    XTX = X.T @ X\\\\n    YTY = Y.T @ Y\\\\n    YTX = Y.T @ X\\\\n    num = np.linalg.norm(YTX, 'fro') ** 2\\\\n    den = np.linalg.norm(XTX, 'fro') * np.linalg.norm(YTY, 'fro')\\\\n    if den == 0:\\\\n        return 0.0\\\\n    return float(num / den)\\\\n\\\\n\\\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\\\n# Activation loading (npy format from extract_activations.py)\\\\n# Returns scalar in [0, 1].\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    X = X - X.mean(axis=0)\\n    Y = Y - Y.mean(axis=0)\\n    XTX = X.T @ X\\n    YTY = Y.T @ Y\\n    YTX = Y.T @ X\\n    num = np.linalg.norm(YTX, 'fro') ** 2\\n    den = np.linalg.norm(XTX, 'fro') * np.linalg.norm(YTY, 'fro')\\n    if den == 0:\\n        return 0.0\\n    return float(num / den)\\n\\n\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n# Activation loading (npy format from extract_activations.py)\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n\\ndef detect_format(act_dir):\\n    p = Path(act_dir)\\n    if list(p.glob(\\\"L*/en_eval.npy\\\")):\\n        return \\\"npy\\\"\\n    if list(p.glob(\\\"layer*_en.pt\\\")):\\n        return \\\"pt_flat\\\"\\n    raise FileNotFoundError(f\\\"No activations found in {act_dir}\\\")\\n\\n\\ndef discover_layers(act_dir, fmt):\\n    p = Path(act_dir)\\n    if fmt == \\\"npy\\\":\\n        return sorted(int(d.name[1:]) for d in p.glob(\\\"L*\\\")\\n                       if (d / \\\"en_eval.npy\\\").exists())\\n    else:\\n        return sorted({int(f.stem.split(\\\"_\\\")[0].replace(\\\"layer\\\", \\\"\\\"))\\n                       for f in p.glob(\\\"layer*_en.pt\\\")})\\n\\n\\ndef discover_languages(act_dir, fmt, layers):\\n    p = Path(act_dir)\\n    langs = set()\\n    if fmt == \\\"npy\\\":\\n        layer_dir = p / f\\\"L{layers[0]}\\\"\\n        for f in layer_dir.glob(\\\"*_eval.npy\\\"):\\n            lang = f.stem.replace(\\\"_eval\\\", \\\"\\\")\\n            if lang != \\\"en\\\":\\n                langs.add(lang)\\n    else:\\n        for f in p.glob(f\\\"layer{layers[0]}_*.pt\\\"):\\n            parts = f.stem.split(\\\"_\\\")\\n            lang = parts[1] if len(parts) >= 2 else None\\n            if lang and lang != \\\"en\\\":\\n                langs.add(lang)\\n    return sorted(langs)\\n\\n\\ndef load_eval_activations(act_dir, fmt, layer, lang):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Load eval-split activations for a language at a layer.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    p = Path(act_dir)\\n    if fmt == \\\"npy\\\":\\n        path = p / f\\\"L{layer}\\\" / f\\\"{lang}_eval.npy\\\"\\n        if not path.exists():\\n            return None\\n        return np.load(path)\\n    else:\\n        import torch\\n        path = p / f\\\"layer{layer}_{lang}.pt\\\"\\n        if not path.exists():\\n            return None\\n        data = torch.load(path, weights_only=True)\\n        acts = data[\\\"activations\\\"].numpy() if hasattr(data[\\\"activations\\\"], 'numpy') else data[\\\"activations\\\"]\\n        n = len(acts)\\n        split = int(n * 0.7)\\n        return acts[split:]\\n\\n\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n# Metrics loading\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n\\ndef load_metrics(metrics_dir, model_name):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Load EN AUROC and ZS AUROC from train_probes.py output.\\n    Returns: en_auroc[layer], zs_aur\\n\\n... zs_auc) / en_auc if en_auc > 0 else 0\\n                cka_vals.append(cka_val)\\n                deg_vals.append(degradation)\\n                zs_vals.append(zs_auc)\\n                langs_used.append(lang)\\n\\n            n_pts = len(cka_vals)\\n            if n_pts < 4:\\n                continue\\n\\n            cka_arr = np.array(cka_vals)\\n            deg_arr = np.array(deg_vals)\\n            zs_arr = np.array(zs_vals)\\n\\n            rho_deg, p_deg = spearmanr(cka_arr, deg_arr)\\n            rho_zs, p_zs = spearmanr(cka_arr, zs_arr)\\n\\n            # Bootstrap CIs\\n            ci_deg = bootstrap_ci((cka_arr, deg_arr), spearman_stat,\\n                                  args.n_bootstrap, seed=args.seed)\\n            ci_zs = bootstrap_ci((cka_arr, zs_arr), spearman_stat,\\n                                 args.n_bootstrap, seed=args.seed)\\n\\n            sig_deg = \\\"*\\\" if p_deg < 0.05 else \\\"\\\"\\n            sig_zs = \\\"*\\\" if p_zs < 0.05 else \\\"\\\"\\n            print(f\\\"{layer:>5d} {n_pts:>3d} {rho_deg:>+10.4f}{sig_deg} {p_deg:>8.4f} \\\"\\n                  f\\\"{rho_zs:>+9.4f}{sig_zs} {p_zs:>8.4f}\\\")\\n\\n            same_layer_corr[layer] = {\\n                \\\"n\\\": n_pts,\\n                \\\"rho_cka_degradation\\\": float(rho_deg),\\n                \\\"p_cka_degradation\\\": float(p_deg),\\n                \\\"ci_cka_degradation\\\": list(ci_deg),\\n                \\\"rho_cka_zs_auroc\\\": float(rho_zs),\\n                \\\"p_cka_zs_auroc\\\": float(p_zs),\\n                \\\"ci_cka_zs_auroc\\\": list(ci_zs),\\n                \\\"languages\\\": langs_used,\\n            }\\n\\n    # ==================================================================\\n    # 2b.
